23, 2012}}\n|gate=$498,650\n|previousevent = [[UFC 99|UFC 99: The Comeback]]\n|followingevent = [[UFC 100]]\n}}\n\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: United States vs. United Kingdom Finale''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 9 Finale''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] [[sporting event|event]] held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC) on June 20, 2009.{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/index.cfm?fa=eventDetail.FightCard&eid=2099 |title=The Ultimate Fighter Team US vs Team UK FINALE |publisher=Ufc.com |access-date=2009-04-09 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20090405094226/http://www.ufc.com/index.cfm?fa=EventDetail.fightCard&eid=2099 |archive-date=2009-04-05 }} Featured were finals from The Ultimate Fighter: United States vs. United Kingdom in both the [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] and [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] divisions as well as a main event between [[Diego Sanchez]] and [[Clay Guida]].\n\n===Background===\nA previously announced lightweight bout between [[Thiago Tavares]] and [[Melvin Guillard]]{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/14430/melvin-guillard-vs-thiago-tavares-set-for-tuf-9-finale.mma |title=Melvin Guillard vs. Thiago Tavares set for The Ultimate Fighter 9 Finale |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |date=April 1, 2009 |first=John |last=Morgan |access-date=April 1, 2009 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20090404024612/http://mmajunkie.com/news/14430/melvin-guillard-vs-thiago-tavares-set-for-tuf-9-finale.mma |archive-date=April 4, 2009 }} was called off due to an elbow injury suffered by Tavares.{{cite web |url=http://mmafrenzy.com/10144/tavares-withdraws-from-tuf-9-finale-bout-against-guillard-due-to-elbow-injury/ |title=Tavares Withdraws From TUF 9 Finale Bout Against Guillard Due To Elbow Injury |publisher=MMAFrenzy.com |date=2008-09-20 |access-date=2009-04-24 |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20090427154607/http://mmafrenzy.com/10144/tavares-withdraws-from-tuf-9-finale-bout-against-guillard-due-to-elbow-injury/ |archive-date=2009-04-27 }} [[Gleison Tibau]] would step in as Tavares' replacement.{{cite web |last=Morgan |first=John |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/14680/gleison-tibau-replaces-thiago-tavares-at-the-ultimate-fighter-9-finale.mma |title=Gleison Tibau replaces Thiago Tavares at The Ultimate Fighter 9 Finale |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |access-date=2009-04-24 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20090427080300/http://mmajunkie.com/news/14680/gleison-tibau-replaces-thiago-tavares-at-the-ultimate-fighter-9-finale.mma |archive-date=2009-04-27 }}\n\n[[Eric Schafer]] was originally scheduled to face [[Tomasz Drwal]] at this event, but he injured his rib and was replaced by Mike Ciesnolevicz.{{cite web |last=Stupp |first=Dann |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/14785/eric-schafer-injured-replaced-by-mike-ciesnolevicz-at-junes-tuf9-finale.mma |title=Eric Schafer injured, replaced by Mike Ciesnolevicz at June's TUF9 Finale |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |access-date=2009-05-05 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20090509065106/http://mmajunkie.com/news/14785/eric-schafer-injured-replaced-by-mike-ciesnolevicz-at-junes-tuf9-finale.mma |archive-date=2009-05-09 }}\n\nA matchup between [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] and [[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]] was scrapped due to a knee injury suffered by Johnson.{{cite web |url=http://mmamania.com/2009/05/19/anthony-johnson-injures-knee-withdraws-from-tuf-9-finale-fight-against-matt-brown/ |title=Anthony Johnson injures knee; Withdraws from TUF 9 Finale fight against Matt Brown |date=19 May 2009 |publisher=Mmamania.com |access-date=2009-05-19 |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110809192709/http://www.mmamania.com/2009/05/19/anthony-johnson-injures-knee-withdraws-from-tuf-9-finale-fight-against-matt-brown/ |archive-date=2011-08-09 }} A replacement was set to be named, however, due to nagging injuries Brown did not fight on the card.{{cite web |last=Morgan |first=John |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/14937/with-anthony-johnson-out-of-tuf-9-finale-matt-brown-follows-suit.mma |title=With Anthony Johnson out of TUF 9 Finale, Matt Brown follows suit |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |access-date=2009-05-20 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20090525091855/http://mmajunkie.com/news/14937/with-anthony-johnson-out-of-tuf-9-finale-matt-brown-follows-suit.mma |archive-date=2009-05-25 }}\n\nThis was the first time in the organization's history, the UFC handed out three Fight of the Night bonuses. Another first for the UFC came in the form of Kim Winslow, the first female referee to officiate a fight in the organization.\n\nThis was the first UFC event to feature five Ultimate Fighter winners on the complete card, including season 9 champions [[Ross Pearson]] & [[James Wilks]], and previous season champions [[Diego Sanchez]], [[Joe Stevenson]], and [[Nate Diaz]]. Only [[UFC 114]] the following year has had the same number of TUF winners since.\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card| Main card (Spike TV)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Diego
